- **prisma-systematic-review**: 1. Protocol: define PICO/SPIDER; write inclusion/exclusion criteria BEFORE searching; pre-register in protocol.md. 2. Search: Boolean queries per database (arXiv, Semantic Scholar, PubMed, IEEE Xplore); log query/date/db/raw-hits in search-log.md. 3. Screen titles/abstracts against criteria; record exclusions with reasons. 4. Full-text screen remaining; log exclusions. 5. - **privacy-opsec-suite-fingerprint-resistance**: Use to reduce the fingerprinting and uniqueness surface that could re-link anonymous users. Timing and size go to traffic-analysis-resistance. - **privacy-opsec-suite-metadata-leak-audit**: Use to find PII or identifiers leaking in logs, telemetry, errors, headers, or file metadata. Timing and size go to traffic-analysis-resistance.
